公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開
曼群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專
業生產各類氣動器材的集團 ， 公司主要從事
生產氣動控制元件 、 氣動執行原件 、 氣源處
理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售等業
務，以 「AirTAC」 自有品牌銷售於大中華地
區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台灣佔 2％、
海外佔 4％ ， 現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動
元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC 在中國市佔
率排名第一，達 33％。

主要競爭對手：SMC、Festo

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

亞德客-KY (1590 TT) 
保持成長趨勢

永豐觀點 
10 月公司日出貨量仍優於前幾個月，4Q26 營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與 3Q26
持平，AI 擴散效應 2027 年將持續發酵，建議逢低買進，區間操作。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議：理由包括：(1) 10 月公司日出貨量仍優於前幾個月，10 月應用領
域中仍有多個領域表現好 ， 這當中應有AI 擴散效應帶來電子業成長的貢獻 。4Q26
營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與 3Q26 持平，(2) 2026 全年維持先前營收成長高於
20%的看法，營業利益率則調高至 35%，氣動元件 2026、2027 年仍處於上升週
期，預估公司營收皆將保持雙位數成長，(3) 公司 2026 年 payout 比例 65%，預
計 2027 年將再提高。評價方面，公司本益比區間 20-40 倍，目前僅 16 倍，處於
相對低檔，AI 擴散效應持續發酵，2026、2027 年獲利在成長軌道上，建議逢低買
進，區間操作，目標價維持 1972 元（27X 2027EPS）。

ESG 評析 
亞德客企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

亞德客為中國第二大氣動元件廠商：亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開曼
群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集團， 公司主要從
事生產氣動控制元件、 氣動執行原件、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售
等業務，以「AirTAC」自有品牌銷售於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台灣
佔 2％、海外佔4％，現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。 
3Q26 EPS 達 14.23 元，符合預期：亞德客 3Q26 營收 115.12 億元 （+39.61%），
營業利益率 33.92%，稅後淨利 28.47 億元（+35.59%YoY），EPS 為 14.23 元，
符合預期。目前中國金屬原物料成本仍在高檔，以加第三季調漲薪資，不過在公司
降本增效的措施持續實施下，第三季營業利益率優於預期。3Q26 有效稅率拉升，
乃因公司有意增加 2027 年以後的 payout 比例 ， 從中國匯出獲利將加徵10%的稅
率。目前氣動元件稼動率高於 100%、線軌稼動率高於 40%，存貨水準 110 天相
對較低（130-140 天屬健康水準） 。 
2026 年全年營收將成長 20%以上，2027 年主要應用領域亦有雙位數成長：過去
氣動元件 2 年一個上升週期，2 年一個下降週期，本波 2025 年開始進入上升週
期，趨勢較溫和，預計成長可超過 2-3 年。2022-2024 年行業雙位數衰退，2025
低個位數成長、預估 2026 將高個位數成長，而 2027 年中位數成長。2026 年以
來公司每月出貨與訂單量皆優於預期 ， 與電子業AI 擴散效應 、 公司規格持續擴增 、
市占率提升等因素有關。行業應用別當中，電子業 3Q26 營收成長 33%，電子業
中 25%-30%與智慧手機有關，主要客人在產品上市前3-6 個月拉貨，預估客戶一
般規格新手機明年上半年上市，故公司 3Q26 手機應用領域成長 26%YoY，預估
可延續至第四季，今年與明年電子行業預估依舊可成長雙位數，此乃因為 AI 擴散
效應將延續；電池行業中國政府有調控政策，9 月公司應收下滑 10%YoY， 不過10
月又恢復 15%-20%的成長，故未來成長幅度仍需觀察，不過中國廠商移至國外擴
廠，對行業成長性仍是有助益；汽車行業公司市占率僅 10%-20%，在市占率持續
提升下，今年與明年雙位數成長；傳統行業如食品、包裝、工具機等受惠於政府補
貼政策，客戶積極擴充自動化產能，以及廠商回到中國擴廠，預估今年與明年依舊
雙位數成長；半導體受惠於中國國產化政策，過去每月營收 500 萬元人民幣，目
前已達 1300-1400 萬元，2027 年新規格上市，4Q26 可讓客戶開始認證，公司規
劃十年後年營收可達 10 億元人民幣。 
線軌市場市況仍在好轉中 ：線軌 2025 出貨量成長 25%，3Q26 營收成長 27%YoY，
今年市場傳動元件市場景氣回升 ， 螺桿大幅調漲價格 ， 線軌景氣也好轉 、 交期拉長 ，
但價格漲幅並不大。公司線軌營收也隨市場水漲船高，目前稼動率 40%（去年
20%+） ，預估年底時可達50%，屆時毛利率可達 30%，稼動率 80%時，毛利率
40%。公司規劃十年後年營收可達 30 億元人民幣。 
電控產品已開始貢獻營收：電控系統中國市場規模 1000 億元人民幣，但有很多不
同系統，公司會挑市場規模大的產品跨入，該產品資本支出低但毛利率高。過去幾
年利用五個系列產品在去年時營收達 4 億元人民幣，主要是搭配氣缸銷售傳感器，

營運現況與分析

### Page 3

3

今年將再推出 3 個系列，今年以來營收成長 20%以上。公司預估未來 10 年公司
有機會做到 30 億元人民幣年營收。 
10 月日出貨金額持續 MoM 成長、4Q26 預計營收可 QoQ 持平：10 月公司日出
貨量仍優於前幾個月，但因工作天數較少，營收不一定會高於 9 月，10 月應用領
域中仍有多個領域表現好 ， 這當中應有AI 擴散效應帶來電子業成長的貢獻 。4Q26
營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與3Q26 持平，2026 全年維持先前營收成長高於 20%
的看法，營業利益率則調高至 35%（先前為 34%） 。至於2027 年，前述氣動元件
主要應用領域皆呈現雙位數的成長，線軌成長預計可超過 20%，在線軌稼動率提
升、原物料平穩下，預計營業利益仍可維持高檔。 
財務預估：預估 2026 年合併營收為 451.46 億元(+31.5％YoY)，稅後淨利 118.13
億元(+40.6％YoY)，EPS 為 59.07 元 。 預估2027 年合併營收為 533.99 億元(+18.28
％YoY)，稅後淨利 143.44 億元(+21.42％YoY)，EPS 為 71.72 元。

市場焦點
美國
• 10月S&P Global綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖較前月回落，但仍處高檔區間；服務業動
能略為放緩，製造業PMI則升至56.0，創近4年半新高。新訂單增速加快 ，顯示企業需求、投
資及庫存回補動能仍具韌性 ，然而企業售價與投入成本同步上升 ，反映需求改善 、供應鏈延
遲及成本轉嫁效應持續存在 ，通膨降溫進程仍具不確定性 。整體而言 ，市場關注焦點轉向通
膨與利率走勢，高利率環境恐維持較長時間，進而推高股債資產評價波動。
中國
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季 GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期 ，在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，
短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建議聚焦在
半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費 、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，

仍需觀察後續刺激政策與消費信心回升情況。
法國
政局動盪
• 法國金融市場因各地抗議活動持續擴大 ，面臨股債雙跌的局面。市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支
出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮 。法國10年期公債殖利率一度突破 5%，法德利差亦擴
大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標仍處於歷史相對低檔 ，遠低於歐
元區主權債務危機期間，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務危機的風險仍相對有限。

美國PMI維持強勁擴張，經濟成長續航但通膨壓力再升溫
• 企業需求強勁支撐美國景氣維持高檔擴張 ：美國10月綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖略低於前月，但仍處於近五年來
的高位區間，顯示企業活動持續高速擴張；其中服務業 PMI為57.9、製造業PMI升至56.0並創53個月新高，新訂單增速
更創逾4年半最佳表現，反映內需穩健、出口需求改善與企業補庫存效應同步發酵，美國第四季經濟動能仍具韌性。
• 成本上升與供應瓶頸推升通膨風險： 企業售價漲幅創2022年8月以來最高，能源、運輸、金屬原料及薪資成本同步上揚 ，
加上供應鏈交期延長與訂單積壓擴大，使企業得以持續轉嫁成本，暗示未來數月消費者通膨可能再度偏離聯準會目標區
間，市場對利率維持較高水準的預期恐因此升溫。
• 後市觀察：景氣韌性有利獲利成長，但通膨可能推升殖利率、壓抑高評價資產。投資配置建議優先布局美國大型科技股 、

美國金融股及工業資本支出受惠族群，可透過NASDAQ 100 ETF、S&P 500 ETF及美國銀行股ETF參與企業獲利成長；
固定收益則以短天期投資級債為主，以降低利率波動風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.24

海外資金流入美國市場，支撐美國資產吸引力
• 海外資金持續流入美國：美國財政部8月TIC資料顯示，
整體資金淨流入 1,998億美元，其中民間部門淨流入
1,785億美元，為主要資金來源；外資淨買進美國長
期證券3,007億美元，遠高於美國投資人加碼海外資
產216億美元，反映全球資金在經濟成長與流動性考
量下，對美國金融市場仍維持高度的配置需求。
• 全球資金偏好美國資產： 外資 8月淨買入美國股票
1,319億美元，高於美國公債與公司債 ，顯示海外投
資人仍看好美國企業獲利與經濟基本面 ，雖然短期國
庫券持有量下降，部分跨境美元資金亦出現波動，但
以10月近況來看長天期公債標售仍吸引境外央行 、
保險資金及大型資產管理機構參與，反映高殖利率水
準對長期資金仍具吸引力。在經濟成長維持韌性與企

業獲利支撐下，美國資產仍為全球資金配置的重要核
心市場。
• 市場看法：海外資金流入有助反映美國資產吸引力，
惟後續仍須觀察通膨 、Fed政策及企業獲利表現 。投
資布局建議可以美國投資等級債、美國中短天期公債
及大型AI基礎建設受惠族群為核心配置 ，兼顧收益、
防禦及長期成長潛力。

歷史數據顯示全球股市突破歷史新高後，後續上漲機率高
• 很多人直覺以為見高就是頂，但過去20年的統計資料顯示，全球股市在創下新高後，多數時間選擇繼續向上走。
• 勝率顯著偏高： 全球股市 (MSCI全球指數 )創新高後持有一年 ，獲得正報酬的機率高達 62%，三個月後的正報酬機率更達
71%。美股與台股表現最亮眼，標普500指數創高後一年上漲機率為76%，那斯達克為79%；而台灣股市表現更為驚人，創
高後三個月內上漲的機率高達82%。
• 下檔拉回風險有限：在創高後短期(一個月內)修正幅度其實相當溫和 ，全球多數主要市場的回徹幅度中位數僅 1%～2%左右。
• 突破前高優於連續創新高： 若市場在歷經至少一個月以上的整理後突破前高 ，其未來一年的平均報酬率高達 8.4%，遠高於

連續每天創新高的3.9%。這代表市場經適度整理後的突破，具備更健康的上漲動能。
創新高後的平均遠期報酬率
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.22

顧
市場寬度收窄，由少數巨頭獨撐
• 當前的市場特徵由少數巨頭帶頭衝： 科技股權重較高的指數(如那斯達克、台股、韓股)，長期以來都有少數權重股拉動指數
的現象。自2024年以來，全球13個主要市場中有10個市場在創新高時，參與上漲的股票比例明顯下降 。已開發市場創新高
時高於200日均線的股票比例從過去 75.5%降至65.1%，新興市場更降至 61.3%。這意味著指數看似風光 ，但多數個股並未
同步創高，當漲勢過度集中於少數大型科技巨頭時，整體市場的抗風險能力會相對減弱。
• 小結與投資建議：歷史經驗顯示，創新高本身是強勢市場的特徵，後續繼續走揚的機率遠大於立刻崩盤。然而，鑑於目前市
場寬度收窄、權重高度集中於科技股的現狀 ，盲目追高個股的風險已顯著增加 。投資建議可以利用ETF掌握大盤趨勢，由於

指數上漲主要由龍頭股驅動 ，透過市值型ETF(如追蹤標普500ETF)能直接享受龍頭巨頭帶來的指數創高紅利 ，避免選錯個股
而賺了指數、賠了價差。此外，提防短期股市回檔，可運用定期定額分批布局或等待大盤適度拉回整理再行加碼，降低追高
風險。
創新高時不同水準的市場寬度佔比

法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓
• 法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓：法國各地抗議活動持續擴大，最初由學生發起，隨後因生活成本高漲而引發其
他群體響應。在財政空間有限、俄烏戰爭與美伊衝突推升能源價格的背景下，政府難以有效緩解民生成本壓力，法國金融市
場因此面臨股債雙跌的局面。
• 公債殖利率攀升，但系統性風險仍相對可控：市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮。法國10
年期公債殖利率一度突破5%，法德利差亦擴大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標（CISS）仍處於
歷史相對低檔，遠低於歐元區主權債務危機期間，顯示金融市場尚未出現廣泛的系統性壓力，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務
危機的風險仍相對有限。

日本央行本週料維持利率不變
日本央行利率走勢預估
資料來源： Bloomberg，2026.10.15 資料來源： tradingeconomics，2026.10.21
日本貿易收支
• 日本貿易逆差擴大，進口增幅高於出口：日本2026年9月貿易逆差擴大至5,366億日圓，較去年同期的2,777億日圓增加93%，
高於市場預期的1,810億日圓，連續第五個月呈現貿易赤字。出口年增16.7%至10.97兆日圓，受惠於半導體相關電子零件、
製造設備及汽車出口成長；惟進口年增18.9%至11.51兆日圓，增幅高於出口，主要受到原油、半導體電子零件及液化天然
氣進口增加帶動。其中，原油進口年增51.1%，部分反映日本在荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張下，積極分散能源採購來源。
• 日本央行本週料維持利率不變：日本央行9月將政策利率調升25個基點至1.25%，創31年來新高。考量前幾次升息對經濟的

傳導效果仍待觀察，加上貿易逆差擴大反映進口成本壓力，多數分析師預期日本央行本週將暫停升息，以評估貨幣緊縮對經
濟與通膨的影響。

中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期：顯示中國經濟在第二季落底後呈現溫和回升 ，從季度表現來看，
一、二、三季增速分別為5.0%、4.3%及4.5%，反映政策支持與外需復甦開始發揮效果 ，全年達成官方4.5%成長目標的機率
明顯提升，第三季經濟改善的主要動能來自出口 ，受惠於全球企業持續擴建 AI基礎設施，及科技產品需求強勁 ，中國高科技
產品出口表現亮眼 ，尤其是積體電路 、伺服器、電腦零組件，及儲存設備等產品增幅顯著 ，高技術製造業維持雙位數成長 。
同時，中國對美國及歐盟出口維持強勁 ，推升貿易順差 走升，為支撐經濟的重要力量 。然而，內需疲弱仍是當前經濟最大隱
憂，房地產調整尚未結束，居民消費與企業投資意願保守，使經濟復甦結構呈現「外熱內冷」的特徵。

• 展望後市：在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建
議聚焦在半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，仍需觀察後續刺激政
策與消費信心回升情況。
資料來源：開源證券，2026.10.22

彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高
• 彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高：彭博產業研究認為，中國AI產業未來將由大型科技平台主導 ，其中騰
訊、字節跳動與阿里巴巴憑藉龐大用戶基礎 、數據資源、資金實力及完整生態系統 ，在AI競賽中具備明顯優勢 ，這些企業可將
AI廣泛應用於電商、社群、遊戲、金融與雲端業務，提升營運效率並強化競爭門檻，例如：阿里巴巴利用 AI 驅動電商平台上的
個人化產品推薦，而騰訊則運用 AI 優化短影音廣告並強化微信功能。騰訊自 2026 年 7 月起開始將混元 AI 模型整合至其電子
遊戲中，使遊戲角色及助理能更準確地回應玩家 ，然而，AI模型訓練與基礎建設投入龐大 ，獲利能力短期仍受壓抑 ，且須面對
監管、資料隱私及晶片供應等挑戰 。此外，智譜、月之暗面、MiniMax及商湯等AI新創雖具技術創新能力，但缺乏規模效益、

穩定現金流及長期資本支持，未來獲利挑戰較大。
• 投資建議：看好中國AI長期發展者，可優先關注具資金 、算力與應用場景優勢的大型平台企業 ，受惠程度可望高於多數 AI新創
公司。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.21

台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高，台股第四季有望先蹲後跳
• 台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高： 9月外銷訂單1,142.1億美元，連兩個月破千億美元 ，再創單月新高；年增率高達 62.4%，連續
20個月呈雙位數正成長 。經濟部表示，AI強勁需求超乎預期 ，預估今年第四季接單還會優於第三季 。展望10月表現，外銷
訂單將持續亮麗，估落在1,125億至1,159億美元，有望連三個月破千億美元。
• 台股第四季有望先蹲後跳： 10月初以來，台股在盤中觸及歷史高點 49,968點後面臨一波修正 ，主要反映在新一輪的財報公
布前，市場觀望AI投資是否存在泡沫化風險 。短期來看，台股在季線附近出現支撐 ，不過在11月初的美國期中選舉落幕前 ，
台股維持區間震盪的可能性較高；中期來看，美國期中選後往往因政治不確定性下降，歷史統計股市上漲機率高，加上台灣

年底集團作帳及選舉行情，正向看待年底台股表現。
資料來源：經濟部統計處，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.20。

実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移
民間最終消費支出 民間企業設備 その他民需 公需 外需
（資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」
( )内は年率換算値
(注）2602までは当研究所による改定見込値、2603は予測値
予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として 輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。 
2026年 7-9月期の地域別輸出数量指数を季節調整
値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％） 、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％） 、アジア向けが前期
比▲0.3％（4-6 月期：同▲0.8％） 、うち中国向けが

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Hon Precision
Company description
Hon Precision is a leading Taiwanese manufacturer specializing in IC
semiconductor automation equipment. Headquartered in Taichung City,
Taiwan, it is most known globally for providing high-precision IC test
handlers, temperature control solutions, and complete automated integrated
solutions for major OSAT and IDM customers.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Hon Precision as we view it sitting in a structurally

protected position at the heart of the AI/HPC back-end test cycle, with
growth visibility extending well into the next few years — the company holds
>90% share of the AI/HPC FT handler market and 60-70% of the ASIC SLT
market. We forecast a solid strong sales revenue CAGR in 2025-28E with
gross margin continuing to expand. In our view, the binding constraint on Hon
Precision's near-term trajectory is now capacity, rather than demand —
placing the company in the early phase of a multi-year structural growth and

margin-expansion cycle.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$10,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 31x applied
to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates. Our PER target of 31x is set at
0.5-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC
testing handler market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR
of > 75% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-25. Our
estimated > 75% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average

(Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing equipment peers. As its peers
have been trading at  average  35x PER on 2027 /28 consensus EPS in the past
1 year, we believe Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target
PER.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price
volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we are not assigning a High
Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of > 75%, significantly stronger

than the growth outlook for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant
position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker capacity build for
semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI
projects; 3) slower-than-expected capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the

company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-
expected development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing
time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak adoption of high-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 1

3Q recap - strong op leverage offset GPM pressure — 3Q26 net profits (+125% y/y) 
topped our/BBG estimates 35%/28% respectively owing mostly to stronger sales 
and larger degree of op leverage. Sales reached a record NT$32.4bn (+24% q/q and 
+83% y/y), 23%/12% above Citi/BBG estimate, which can be attributable to strong 
demand in edge AI, semi equipment and smart healthcare applications. GPM fell to 
36.7% (-1ppt q/q and -2.2ppt y/y) below Citi/BBG estimates of 38.0%/37.6%, 
reflecting higher component prices. Nevertheless, OPM expanded to 20.9%, 
underpinned by strong op leverage on higher sales scale. OP profits +151% y/y beat 
Citi/BBG forecast 33%/21%, the key driver to the earnings beat.    
Implications — Strong order intake is encouraging with 3Q BB ratio reaching 1.6x 
and NA particularly robust at 2.1x. However, we believe the stock's rich valuation 
limits further re-rating potential, particularly as a higher comparison base could 
moderate growth in 2027E. Continued GPM compression also reinforces our 
concerns over material/component inflation and the timing of price increases to 
offset higher costs. Advantech will host 3Q26 results call on Nov 4th. We will revisit 
our numbers and share key takeaways following management's updates.

Advantech
Valuation
Our target price of NT$670 is based on 35x 2H26-1H27E EPS, at the high-cycle of the stock's valuation range between 20-35x
since 2020, reflecting solid BB ratio, improving margin profile and ROE.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause Advantech’s shares to trade above our target price include: 1) better-than-expected
marcoeconomic outlook; and 2) better-than-expected demand and margin profile. Key downside risks would include: 1)

slower-than-expected demand recovery, especially the China market; and 2) further deterioration of demand in US and EU
markets.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: ASE disclosed a strong set of quarterly results and guidance 
on 29 October, both beating market expectations, thanks to the strong AI 
scaling which is just getting stronger to benefit ASE’s leading-edge 
advanced packaging (LEAP) business. We raise our forecasts and 12M TP 
and reaffirm our Outperform (2) call on the stock; accumulate into any 
technical pullback.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. ASE reported 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD27bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 25%/18% 
thanks to higher semiconductor assembly & test (SAT, or IC ATM by 
company definition) revenue which came in at TWD151bn (USD4.7bn), 7% 
above our estimate, for higher gross margin (GM) of 30.5%, 1.9pp above 
ours and topping ASE’s structural target of 24-30%. The strength was 
amplified further by lower non-operating losses, resulting in the strong 
bottom-line beat (table on p.2). Guidance was another beat thanks to the 
strong SAT business where ASE expects revenue to grow 11-13% QoQ for 
GM to top its record of 30.5% during the COVID upturn. We raise our 2026-
28E EPS by 14-24% assuming its SAT GM to reach 33% through 2027.

Strong AI gets stronger. Discussions centred on ASE’s LEAP business, 
where management further hiked revenue target this year beyond USD4bn 
(USD1.6bn in 2025), after 3 hikes YTD, thanks to strong demand for its on-
substrate (oS) packaging and initial ramp of its full-process solutions. With 
addition of new co-packaged optics (CPO) solutions, ASE expects the 
LEAP revenue to double YoY in 2027. As LEAP is margin-accretive, ASE 
expects the SAT GM to continue improving beyond 4Q26, making the 
ceiling of its structural GM target now as the floor to kick off 2027 for a 
strong start. Given management’s emphasis on “friendly pricing”, we expect 
likely continued cost passthrough across the supply chain to add to GM 
upside. Working hard to narrow the supply/demand gap, ASE further raised 
this year’s capex to USD11.5bn (from USD10.5bn), reinforcing our view 
that AI is scaling strongly to accelerate the chip industry growth.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for ASE to TWD710 (previous: TWD550), based on our revised ROE-
adjusted PBR target of 5.8x (previous: 5.2x) 2027E BVPS due to a higher 
ROE trajectory. Our revised TP translates into 24x 4-quarter forward PER, 
which we see as justifying the rich PBR target given its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 25%. We reiterate our Outperform (2) call with key downside risks 
of any “silicon inflation” and geopolitical overhang leading to AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 5-10% above the consensus as we 
believe the street has yet to factor in its stronger LEAP business.

29 October 2026

Entering an even stronger earnings cycle (III)

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Strong AI scaling is getting even stronger to benefit LEAP 
➢ Reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD710

Company profile 
Established in April 2018, ASE Industrial Holding Co (HoldCo) is a holding company which privatized 
ASE and SPIL fully on 30 April 2018 through equity and cash swap, respectively, and listed on the same 
day with senior management assigned mainly by ASE. HoldCo has become the strong No.1 OSAT 
maker in the world, offering a comprehensive product portfolio to its customers from wirebonding, flip-
chip & bumping, system-in-package (SiP) to broad-based electronics manufacturing services (EMS).

### Page 5

5

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
ASE’s Board of Directors consists of nine members with diverse professional backgrounds.
Three of the directors are independent, representing 33 percent of the board. The Chairman also
serves as the Chief Executive Officer, resulting in unified leadership and centralized decision-
making. To maintain effective governance and oversight under this structure, ASE relies on its
independent directors and established board processes. The board currently includes one
female director, and the company continues to pursue long-term goals related to board diversity
and balanced representation. ASE has an Audit Committee, Compensation Committee, Risk
Management Committee, and Chief Government Officer to conduct effective supervision.
Capital management 3
In the past 5 years, ASE paid over 50% or nearly 50% of its earnings in dividends. However, the
dividend trend was volatile in dollar and payout ratio terms. We forecast its dividend payout ratio
to average over 50% in the next 2 years.
Related party &
transaction 2
ASE undertakes related-party transactions with its subsidiaries on sales and purchases. The
total related-party transaction sales/purchase amounts were less than 10% of its total
sales/purchases, and the transaction terms with related parties were normal compared with
those with other customers/suppliers. Thus, we do not think related-party transactions have a
meaningful impact on overall operations.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
The supply of raw materials has the most direct impact on ASE's operations and manufacturing.
To lower overall supply-chain risks, ASE has expanded the scope of sustainability risk
management to non-tier-1 suppliers. ASE currently has over 600 non-tier-1 suppliers.
ASE requires its suppliers to comply with its Supplier Code of Conduct, which aims to ensure
that suppliers conduct business in a manner that meets labour, health & safety, environment,
business ethics & management, and various corporate compliance standards.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
ASE has adopted 3 water-use strategies of reduce, reuse and recycle. Through its water
management measures, it reduced water consumption density (water consumption/revenue) by
43% in 2024 vs. that of 2015. ASE is committed to increasing water reuse at its facilities in order
to improve water efficiency.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
ASE is committed to improving waste recycling and reuse through the 5 key elements of a
circular economy of: direct recycling, reuse, off-site regeneration, renewable materials, and
supply and rental. The recycling rate of general waste was 90% in 2024 and its hazardous waste
density declined by 53% in 2024 vs. that of 2015.
E GHG emissions 1
ASE is committed to achieving net zero emissions by advancing low carbon energy adoption,
developing smart grid technologies, and strengthening high tech energy management systems.
As of 2024, 19% of total manufacturing energy consumption comes from renewable sources,
88% of ASE facilities use renewable energy, and 10 sites have achieved RE100 certification.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on AVC. The company plans to release 
its 3Q26 results on 10 November. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our 
latest memo Record-high 3Q26 revenue beat expectations, 7 October). We 
expect AVC to maintain its leading position in the following years with more 
content value from ASICs projects since 4Q26.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results preview. AVC’s 3Q26 revenue of 
TWD59,089m (+20.3% QoQ and +51.8% YoY), a record-high level, 
accounted for 102.3% and 103.0%, respectively, of our previous 
(TWD57,771m, +17.6% QoQ and +48.4% YoY) and the Bloomberg 
consensus forecasts for 3Q26 revenue. We forecast its 3Q26 gross margin 
to be 33.5% (vs. 32.6% in 2Q26 and 26.1% in 3Q25), slightly higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus forecast of 33.3%. We forecast its operating 
profit of TWD16.8bn (+25.0% QoQ and +108.3% YoY), 4.5% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus forecast of TWD16.1bn and we forecast its 
operating margin of 28.5% (vs. 27.4% in 2Q26 and 20.8% in 3Q25). 
Overall, we forecast its net profit of TWD11.6bn (+21.6% QoQ, +117.8% 
YoY), 4.3% higher than the consensus estimate of TWD11.2bn. For 4Q26, 
we forecast revenue to grow 15.1% QoQ (monthly revenue to rise MoM), 
and 1Q27 revenue to grow 2.9% QoQ considering ASIC and GPU projects.

Solid capacity expansion plan in 2027; worsening supply-demand 
environment to bring in a more favourable price trend to the sector. In 
light of the manifold content value from trays, we now expect content value 
by one of its ASICs clients to increase by another 20%. AVC expects the 
revenue drivers in 2027 to include double-sided cold plates, rising liquid 
cooling penetration from ASICs, and more content value wins (one-stop 
shopping model), for example, from CDU, CPU and NPO. As such, AVC 
plans to ramp up its cold plate capacity by 60% by 3Q27 with more 
automated product lines (positive to its further gross margin YoY 
expansion) to support its Vietnam plants, based on current order visibility 
and high utilisation rate. We keep our 2026-28E EPS unchanged.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD4,815 (from TWD4,465), still based on a target PER of 30x vs. a 
past-3-year PER range of 5-25x [average PER of 15x]) on its solid earnings 
visibility (earnings CAGR of 45.3% over 2026-2028E; 0.66x PEG) and 
applied to our 1-year forward EPS. Downside risks: slower-than-expected 
progress in high-gross-margin orders, slow AI server adoption, and 
competition.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above consensus, likely as we are 
relatively more aggressive in our revenue, gross and operating margins.

Company profile 
AVC is a global leading comprehensive thermal solutions provider in segments related to desktops, 
laptops, communication, servers, energy and autos. Its main product line includes thermal products 
(thermal modules and fans), chassis, system assembly and peripheral products and hinges. The 
company has been listed on Taiwan’s main board since September 2002.

Asia Vital Components (3017 TT): 2 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 6 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson of AVC are the same individual, but we see no issue with its board
quality. Besides, we see the Chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry, which is a
positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 2 AVC's dividend payout ratio was 40-52% over 2020-25, likely due to its capacity expansionplans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was 1-3% of total revenue during 2021-25,and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chainmanagement 1
AVC has introduced Hazardous Substance Process Management (HSPM) and received
QC080000 certification. In addition, AVC set up a Green Product team to promote HSPM and
formulate environmental regulation to make sure its material sourcing and efficiency are well-
performed.
E Data security 1
To prevent the interruption of information systems and leakage of sensitive information causing
losses to the company and customers, AVC has introduced an information security management
system and passed the ISO 27001 information security management system certification in
2019.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 7

Asia Vital Components (3017 TT): 2 November 2026

Document 160: 261102_ms_GUC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261102_ms_GUC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 15
Status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
Axion CPU demand remains strong and on track: We noted  in a prior report  that
demand for Google's Axion CPUs, which GUC helps design, remains robust.
However, we now see further upside in both 2027 and 2028 from our previous
forecast. Also, some investors have questioned whether the Axion3 schedule has
been pushed out. Our  recent supply chain checks suggest that Axion3 has already
reached wafer-in and is on track for wafer-out in mid-2027. Axion CPU could reach 4-

5mn units of  Axion2 equivalent CPU in 2027, given that Axion3 includes two
computing dies but also adopts TSMC 3nm process. We now forecast volume at
4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3 in 2027, then 3mn units of Axion3 in
2028. We project ASP at US$700 and US$1400, respectively.
We also see upside surprise from Cobalt CPU: We believe the CPU demand upside
is coming not just from Axion but also Microsoft's Cobalt CPU. GUC continues to
get more front-end wafer support from TSMC; given strong CPU demand, we now

forecast >1mn units of Cobalt 200 in 2027 with ASP of ~US$700. Our industry
checks also suggest that Cobalt 300 is on track for mass production in 2028, which
might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die
CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be ~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
3Q26 revenue beat from CPU customers' demand pull-forward and new
customer: The company posted its  3Q26 results: revenue beat our estimate

strongly (by 23%);  the upside comes mostly from the largest CPU customer's pulling
demand forward  (50-55% of 3Q26 total revenue) and also the new US AI customer,
which has adopted the 5nm process and SoW (system-on-wafer) at TSMC. In 4Q26,
we expect a major Chinese auto company to start to contribute revenue. With the
ongoing robust CPU demand, we now expect 4Q26 revenue to be up 20% QoQ. See
Exhibit 3  for our 3Q26 earnings review.
Reiterate OW and raise price target to NT$8600: This 37% increase mostly

reflects the upside we have seen from CPU demand. However, aside from CPU
demand, we also see strong revenue from Tesla, Meta, Horizon, etc.   in 2028. Thus,
we expect 2025-28 revenue CAGR to reach 120%. Our new price target implies P/E
of 52x for 2027e and 27x for 2028e, which we think is justified by its strong revenue
growth.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Strong chip demand from CPU  and AI business
Axion CPU should be the largest customer for GUC in 2026-2028: We therefore now
forecast only 1.5mn units of Google Axion2 CPU in 2026, given the demand pull-in. By
assuming chip ASP at ~US$700, we now see 2026 Google CPU contributing slightly more
than US$1000mn.
For 2027, we have been seeing strong rises in demand expectations for Axion CPU,
including  the ramp of the next-generation Axion3. We now expect Axion CPU revenue of

close to US$3.5bn, including 4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3. In addition,
we also expect the chip ASP of Axion3 to double from Axion2 given the increase of 3nm
computing dies from one to two.
For 2028, we now forecast  3mn units of Axion3, slightly lower than Axion2's annual run
rate of 4mn units, mostly also driven by the doubling of computing dies.
CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin for Axion CPU – likely around 10% –
because this is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design

support (e.g., silicon interposer and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for
the ASIC designs. We also expect margin for Axion3 to be slightly lower than for Axion2,
given higher chip ASP or larger project size.
Cobalt demand could  also grow 3-4x in 2027: GUC also helps with Microsoft's Cobalt
200/300 CPU. We now forecast at least US$1bn of GUC revenue contribution from
Cobalt 200 and Maia 200 in 2027. Also, our industry checks also suggest that Cobalt 300

is on track for mass production in 2028, which might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By
assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be
~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
Meta ASIC (MTIA 600): In our AI supply chain tracker, we noted our belief that GUC
could win one of the Meta ASIC projects, led by the Rivos team, and we now think it might
be MTIA 600, based on supply chain checks. The AI ASIC targets tape-out in 1H27, with

potential CoWoS out in 2028.
Note: these forecasts are from Asia Pacific research and incorporate supply chain and
industry checks.

Estimate revision summary
We raise   our EPS estimates 17% for 2026, 18% for 2027, and 50% for 2028: Our
increases come mostly from the strength in CPU as well as other AI projects' contribution,
including Meta ASIC, US design house adopting SoW, etc. With turnkey revenue share
seen increasing in both 2027 and 2028, we now assume that gross margin will fall from
21.4% in 2026 to 15.4% in 2028. However, we expect firmer operating margin this year,

thanks to a policy change regarding employee bonus; although this assumption dips in
2027, it rebounds in 2028 because of larger revenue scale with better operating leverage.
The same trend is visible in  net margin.

Price target rises 37%, to NT$8,600
This reflects our 2027-28 estimate revisions. Although we are cutting our terminal growth
rate assumption from 5% to 4.5% because of margin dilution, our other  key valuation
assumptions are unchanged, including  cost of equity of 9.2% (a beta of 1.2, a risk premium
of 6%, and a risk-free rate of 2%), and intermediate growth rate  of 14.5%.
Our bull and bear case values rise by similar magnitudes, to   NT$11,680  and NT$5,300,

respectively.

Relative valuation
Our price target of NT$8,600 implies 52x our 2027 EPS estimate, around +1 SD from
GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market

will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term, and therefore believe
that further re-rating would be justified with project wins likely to come. Despite slower
recovery of non-AI business, we continue to appreciate GUC’s growing exposure to cloud
AI semis (including AI accelerators, AI server CPU, and BMC chips).

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

주가수익률 (%) 1개월 3개월 12개월  
 절대 (6.6) (10.0) (6.5)  
 상대 8.0  (14.2) (35.7)  
 절대 (달러환산) (5.2) (2.7) 0.2

3Q26 영업이익 1.8조, 핵심 하락 원인은 인센티브 
환율효과는 전분기 대비 -5,330억 작용했으나, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비의 급감 
(6,200억, -9,250억 qoq)으로 상쇄되었다 판단. 인센티브 비용 증가세는 -6,060억원의 영
향을 미쳤으며, 대부분 유럽지역 EV2 판매를 위한 딜러 인센티브에서 발생 추정. 
외생변수는 능력 밖의 일, EV 전략에 대해서는 장기성장에 무게 
중동 분쟁 지속, 환율 하락과 같은 외생변수의 부정적 영향을 제외한다면 단기 영업적 측면
의 실적 악재는 BEV 인센티브 지급기조의 변화가 없을 것이라는 점. 중장기 글로벌 BEV 
시장의 입지 강화를 단기 전사 수익성보다 중시하는 전략을 채택. 
마찬가지로, 4분기가 환율 효과의 본격화 
3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (QTD 1,338원) 효과로 환관련 
감익 본격화 예상. 즉, 판매보증비의 정상화 (qoq 반등)로 인해 수익성 추가 악화 가능성 존
재. 완성차 판매량의 경우 경쟁사 대비 견조했던 2Q-3Q26의 기조를 유지할 것이라 예상. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 129,000원으로 하향 
목표주가 하향 근거는 산정 기준을 FY27F로 변경함과 동시에 FY27F 자동차사업 NOPAT 
예상치 하향과 현대모비스 지분가치 하락. 밸류에이션상 저점에 도달했다는 의견은 FY27F 
EPS의 yoy 하락으로 설득력 약화. 중장기 BEV 시장 입지 강화에 대한 밸류에이션 프리미
엄 반영은 글로벌 전동화 속도에 달려 있음. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) 외생변수의 개선, 2) 신
차 출시를 통한 상품성 강화, 3) BEV 시장 점유율 확대 이후 점진적 인센티브 축소, 4) 20조
원 수준의 순현금 기반 발생 가능한 사업적 결정.

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
여전히 가격 상승 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 95.8조원, 영업이익 75.1조원으로 예상한다. 
이전 전망 대비 감소한 수준이다. 원/달러 환율 영향이다. 영업이익률은 78.4%로 전 
분기 대비 2.1%p 상승할 전망이다. DRAM 매출액은 73.1조원, 영업이익 
59.2조원으로 추정한다. DRAM ASP는 이전 전망 대비 상향 조정했다. 일부 제품 
가격 인상을 반영한 영향이다. Bit Growth는 이전 전망 수준을 유지했다. ASP 
상향이 수익성 개선에 미친 영향은 크지 않을 전망이다. NAND 매출액은 22.4조원, 
영업이익은 15.9조원으로 예상한다. 
Global Top 수준 주주환원 규모/자사주 살 게 더 있다 
SK하이닉스는 2026년 8월 19일 주주환원에 대한 새로운 정책을 제시했다. FCF에서 
CAPEX를 제외한 금액의 50% 이상을 주주환원에 사용한다는게 핵심이다. 2026년 
기준으로 최소 90조원 수준일 것으로 추정한다. 자사주 중심으로 주주환원을 계획 
중이고 이미 집행 중인 40조원 이외에 4분기 중에 추가로 자사주 매입이 진행될 
것으로 예상한다. 배당도 일부 집행이 되겠지만 자사주가 중심이 될 전망이다. 
흔들리지 말아야 할 이유는 많다 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 모델의 성장으로 메모리에 
대한 수요가 지속적으로 높아지고 있고, 이에 비해 공급 증가는 제한적인 상황이 
상당기간 지속될 것으로 전망하기 때문이다. 이로 인해 현재 수준의 DRAM, NAND 
가격에 변동성은 낮고, 높은 수익성이 유지되면서 안정적 현금흐름 창출이 가능할 
것으로 전망한다. 현재 주가는 지나치게 저평가되어 있는 국면에 있다고 판단한다. 
목표주가는 12개월 예상 EPS 446,092원에 PER 9 배를 적용한 4,000,000원을 
유지한다.

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 건설/원전 | 2026. 10. 30  
숫자로 증명하는 중

3Q26 Review: 에너빌부문 믹스개선, 두산밥캣 호실적 
두산에너빌리티 3분기 매출액은 4조 4,494억원으로 전년동기대비 14.7% 증가, 컨센
서스에 부합했다. 영업이익은 2,612억원으로 전년동기대비 90.6% 증가, 컨센서스 영
업이익 2,464억원 대비 6.0% 상회하는 호실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티부문의 원자
력, 가스터빈 매출 증가에 따른 믹스 개선 효과와 두산밥캣의 가격정책 효과에 힘입은 
마진 개선으로 안정적인 실적 기조가 이어지고 있어 긍정적이다. 분기 환평가손실로 
인해 순이익은 여타 수출기업과 유사하게 컨센서스 대비 하회했다. 에너빌부문의 수주
는 누계 10조원으로 목표대비 75%를 채웠으며, 수주 대기중인 안건들을 고려할 때 
당분간 수주 업사이클이 몇년간 지속될 것임이 분명한 시점이다. 
기회가 아닌 경쟁력, 모멘텀이 아닌 수주  
두산에너빌리티에 대해 투자의견 Buy 유지하나, 목표주가를 95,000원으로 하향했다. 
목표 EBITDA는 2035년 EBITDA에 WACC 8%를 적용, 목표 EV/EBITDA는 Global 
Peers인 GE Vernova의 2026년 EV/EBITDA 멀티플에서 자회사 실적 변동성을 고
려해 10% 할인 적용했다. 호실적과 수주 증가에도 GEV의 멀티플 감소와 순차입금 
증가로 인한 NAV 감소를 밸류에이션에 반영한 결과이다. 전일 주가는 한수원의 체코 
원전 시공사 협상 결렬로 인해 7.65% 하락 마감했다. 시공 수주는 아쉽지만 두산에너
빌리티의 체코 원전 기자재 납품에는 전혀 영향이 없으며, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가로 경
제적해자인 대형 가스 터빈 수급 쇼티지는 당분간 지속될 전망임을 고려할 때 전일 주
가는 낙폭 과대로 판단한다. 1) 두산에너빌리티의 가스터빈은 이미 2031년 출하 물량
에 대한 예약 주문이 마무리되고 있으며, 2) 또한 AP1000의 우량한 수주 파이프라인
과, 3) 뉴스케일파워, 테라파워, 롤스로이스, X Energy 등의 SMR 제작을 위한 협업 역
시 지속되고 있다는 점에 주목한다. 전일 기사를 통해 뉴스케일파워 SMR 1호 제작 계
약을 체결했다는 소식을 전했으며, 이제 에너지 트렌지션은 기회가 아닌 회사의 경쟁
력으로, 가스터빈과 원전은 모멘텀이 아니라 수주로 입증되고 있다.  
 Analyst 김세련 
sally.kim@ls-sec.co.kr

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book 1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.
Investment Implications
Oil market conditions remain a swing
factor for the outlook. A shift lower in
oil risk premia and improvement on
the refining front would go a long way

toward easing inflation and interest
rates while bolstering risk-asset
appetite. A gradual increase in Fixed
Income portfolio duration continues to
make sense, in our view.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.
Exhibit 1: While Equities Have Weathered the Structural Increase in Interest Rates Since 2020, Many Have Struggled in the
Face of Growing Exogenous Oil Shock Effects on Inflation and Rates.

A) Increase in real long-term interest rate since 2020 mostly structural. However, much of this
year’s increase appears due to oil-price effects.
B) Rising oil prices have restrained equity-market rally.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings hav e generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than tw o-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.” 2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market. 2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of inv estment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractiv e in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market mo ves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting go vernment
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff
Source: Chief Investment Office. Data as of October 22, 2026.
* “Pop the Champagne,” Portfolio Insights, Chief Investment Office, August 2026.

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

動態更新  科技‧美國

Vertiv 
(VRT.N/ VRT US)

Onecore、CDU與 800VDC為三大推升營運加速成長之動能 
重要訊息 
Vertiv  FY3Q26 獲利優於市場共識 21%，公司上修 FY2026 EPS 財測約 7%
至年增 70%，來自資料中心散熱與電源需求升溫， 收盤股價上漲約 16%。 
評論及分析  
FY3Q26 EPS 年增 78%至 2.21 美元、優於市場共識 22%。Vertiv  FY3Q26 
(2026 年 7-9 月)營收年增 61%、季增 32%至 43.1 億美元，主要受惠資料
中心需求升溫，以及 FY2Q26 因供應端問題遞延之營收約 1-1.5 億美元已於
本季認列。FY3Q26 營利率年增 2.9ppts、季增 2.6ppts 至 25.2%，主要受
惠營利率較佳的美洲地區成長較強勁 ；EPS 年增 78%、季增 45%至 2.21 美
元，優於凱基預估 15%、市場共識預估 22%。 
Vertiv 上修 FY2026 EPS 財測約 7%至 7.15 美元。Vertiv  展望 FY4Q26 營
收年增 53%、季增 2%至 44.1 億美元，若還原 FY3Q26 遞延營收之貢獻，
則兩個季度的營收年增率相當； EPS 財測為年增 65%、季增 2%至 2.25 美
元，大致符合市場共識預估。基於資料中心需求升溫， 公司上調 FY2026 營
收約 5%至 146.5 億美元，隱含年增 43%；EPS 財測上修 7%至 7.15 美
元，隱含年增 70%，優於市場共識預估 7%。 
凱基預估 Vertiv  FY2026/2027 EPS 為 7.32/11.89 美元，年增 62%。
Vertiv 在 2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售
價將較 1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美
元，而目前僅三家供應商通過認證 ，凱基預期此將有助於公司提升 CDU 供
貨份額，並據此上修 FY2026/FY2027 EPS 自原預估之 6.91/10.94 美元至
7.32/11.89 美元，年增 62%。 
投資建議  
評等維持「增加持股」，目標價 416 美元 (35x FY2027F EPS) 。 
投資風險 
AI 晶片訂單遞延； CSP 廠放緩資本支出增幅；關稅政策變化影響生產成本。

美國 Vertiv

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Vertiv 營收主要來自資料中心基礎建設解決方案 。Vertiv 業務為提供資料中
心基礎建設解決方案， 營收約 75%來自資料中心客戶。Vertiv 產品組合可分
為硬體 (Products ； FY2Q26 營收占比 80%) 及服務與備品 (Services & 
Spares；20%) 兩大項目。其中  (1) 硬體：提供電源管理、熱管理及整合性
機櫃解決方案，包括交流不斷電供應系統 (UPS)、冷卻系統等；(2) 服務與備
品包含預防性維護、工程和諮詢服務、遠程監控、備件等。FY 2Q26 各地區
營收比重為美洲  68%、亞太地區  19%、歐非中東地區 13%。獲利方面，
美洲基於 AI 資料中心規格要求較高 ，營利率 達 25%-30%、優於公司平
均；歐洲近幾季因關稅成本上揚而使營利率降至 15%-20%；亞太地區因價
格競爭激烈，營利率僅約 10%-15%、低於平均。  
Vertiv 提供完整電源與散熱系統 解決方案，協助客戶建置資料中心 。相較於
散熱或電源廠同業大多僅提供零組件， Vertiv 擁有完整產品線與提供系統建
置的能力；基於下一代 AI 語言模型的運算量與傳輸需求皆顯著提升，使散
熱整合與解決方案的設計難度也隨之向上，讓 Vertiv 相較於僅能提供單一零
組件的廠商更有競爭力。 另外，因應資料中心停機造成損失的問題，Vertiv
也推出 Next Predict ，透過即時遙測與 AI 演算法持續監控 BESS 電池儲能系
統、UPS 與液冷 CDU/泵浦的運作狀態，在異常影響 AI 算力前先行預警，從
而預防損失。Vertiv 資料中心客戶包含 AWS、Meta、Microsoft 、AT&T、
Verizon 、Coreweave 、Oklo，供應模式大多是將電源、散熱與系統等產品
一併提供給客戶，協助其建置資料中心。  
在產品接單上，凱基預期 Vertiv 推出之 OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案將
對整體訂單具有高度貢獻。此解決方案主要是因應伺服器液冷散熱與電源需
求所提供的產品，其採用直接接觸晶片散熱 (Direct -to-Chip Cooling) 技術，
避免晶片在電力故障的幾十秒內因失去冷卻而受損 ，提供建置容量從 10MW
到 1,000MW 的選擇。OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案 之中的 MegaMod ™ 
HDX 主要是針對需要建造一座小型資料中心的客戶所設計，因應數十櫃至數
百櫃的 AI 資料中心建置需求。基於 AI 伺服器機房的液冷散熱管路與電力分
配較為複雜，若由不同供應商安裝可 能會出現漏水或電力供應不足的狀況；
Vertiv 可解決此整合問題，並將安裝時間壓縮在 9-12 個月，相較於大多數
供應商須 18-24 個月而言更為迅速。 目前市場上已有超過 500 家公司有自
建小型資料中心的需求，若以 10MW 方案價格約 8,000 萬美元評估，潛在
市場產值達 400 億美元以上，而觀察目前有提供同等級產品者僅
Schneider( 法)與 Eaton  (美)等同業，使 Vertiv 可望在此領域成為主要供應商
之一， 凱基預期公司 將取得超過 130 億美元訂單，有利於公司
FY2026/FY20 27 營收維持高成長性。  
Vertiv 在 2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售
價將較 1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美
元，目前具相同提供能力的供應商僅包含韓國 LG 電子與美國 LiquidStack ，
Vertiv 可望在 CDU 供應鏈提升份額，預期公司可在此市場取得約 20%供貨
份額，並可望貢獻 2027 年營收達 15 億美元。

### Page 3

美國 Vertiv

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

圖 1: Vertiv推出 2.3MW CDU，預估貢獻 2027年營收達 15億美元

資料來源：公司資料；凱基

Nvidia 與 ASIC AI 伺服器陸續導入電源櫃，預計 FY2027 顯著貢獻營收。
基於 Nvidia 與 CSP 廠下世代 AI 伺服器功耗皆顯著提升，客戶對於能處理達
200kW 熱能的液冷散熱需求增加，推升散熱產值提升。另外，因各地政府
對 PUE 規範逐步往 1.5 以下的水準靠攏，而現行氣冷散熱之 PUE 普遍落在
1.7 上下的水準，此限制亦使客戶對散熱方案產生升級需求。凱基 預期在 AI
散熱解決方案升級、MegaMod ™ HDX 營收貢獻逐步增加、以及公司可望在
2.3MW CDU 市場提升份額的帶動下，Vertiv FY2026/FY2027 散熱產品營收
可望達 60.9/92.0 億美元，年增 51%。 
電源產品部分，Vertiv  800VDC 產品目前處於驗證階段 ，預計 2027 年開始
貢獻營收，合作對象包含 hyperscaler 及晶片端夥伴。基於其單價約達 50 萬
美元、預估 2027 年出貨量約落在 1,000- 2,000 櫃上下的水準；公司目前亦
有推出 400VDC 電源櫃，初期以 Meta 和 Microsoft 導入較為積極，預期電
源櫃產品可望貢獻 Vertiv  FY2027 營收達 10 億美元，為其 2027 年電源產品
線主要成長動能，預估 Vertiv FY2026/FY2027 電源產品營收為 36.5/60.7 億
美元，年增 66%。

Vertiv 評等維持「增加持股」，目標價為 416 美元。Vertiv 在資料中心散熱
和電源產品提供的 solution 比大多數同業更完整，營收成長性可望優於產業
平均；凱基預期公司推出的 OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案將拿下超過
130 億美元訂單， 此為支撐公司營運成長之主要動能。另外，公司 在
2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售價將較
1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美元，而目
前僅三家供應商通過認證，凱基預期此將有助於公司提升 CDU 供貨份額，
並據此上修 FY2026/FY2027 EPS 自原預估之 6.91/10.94 美元至 7.32/11.89
美元，年增 62%；相較市場共識 EPS 分別為 6.66/9.23 美元，目前股價交易
於約 22/26 倍之 FY2027 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Vertiv 過往五年本益比多落於 12-36 倍之間，考量 (1) Vertiv 在電源與散熱
產品之整合能力優於同業表現，並能提供系統級產品，有助公司接單能 力提
升；(2) ASIC 晶片陸續推出且對電源與散熱需求皆提升，可望推升公司未來
兩年獲利成長性優於產業平均；(3) 公司在 2.3MW CDU 與 800VDC 領域皆
為少數供應商之一， FY2027 電源與散熱產品成長性可望進一步提升。綜合
上述原因，預期 Vertiv 本益比將回升至區間上緣之 35 倍上下浮動；基於當
前評價偏低，故維持「增加持股」之 評等，目標價則基於預估 EPS 上修而調
升至 416 美元 (35x FY2027F EPS )。

圖 5: 公司概況  圖 6: 公司 2025年美洲營收占比達 62% 
Vertiv 前身為 Emerson Network Power ，並於 2016 年開始獨
立運營。公司專注於為資料中心、通訊網路 以及商業與工業環
境提供關鍵基礎設施與服務。產品與服務涵蓋交流與直流電源
管理、不間斷電源系統（ UPS）、電源分配單元（PDU ）、熱
管理解決方案、資料中心基礎設施管理（ DCIM）以及服務
等。2025 年各地區營收比重為美洲 62%、亞太 20%、歐非
中東 18%。

主題報告概述 : 本篇報告將分析 2026年美元現況 ，最終形成
2027年美元看法。以下為本篇報告內容概要:
(1) 匯市現況：美國伊朗戰爭導致通膨疑慮再起，美元走強。
(2) 政治議題：2026年共和黨失去絕對執政對美元的影響。
(3) 期中選後風險因子：財政、貿易戰、美伊戰爭。
(4) 美元基本面：景氣與通膨循環將維持在金髮女孩經濟環境。
(5) 美元技術面：技術面跌破年線，籌碼面偏多但8月轉弱。
(6) 非美匯市: 歐洲政治面利空及日本阻貶為美元提供底部支撐
匯市結論: 美元已從2026年初的降息交易轉向受到美伊戰爭 、

油價上漲及通膨預期升溫支撐 ，但此波強勢更多來自地緣政治
與高利率環境 ，而非美國基本面出現結構性轉強 。期中選舉後 ，
共和黨失去完全執政優勢 ，國會分裂將限制大規模財政擴張 ，
有助抑制通膨與赤字惡化 ，但也降低進一步刺激經濟成長的動
能。展望2027年，美國仍面臨債務上限 、財政協商、貿易政策、
美伊衝突及烏俄戰爭等風險 ，其中地緣政治與油價走勢仍是左
右美元的重要變數 。2027年美元並非進入新一輪強勢多頭週期 ，
而是在高利率支撐下維持區間整理 。2027年美國經濟基準情境
穩健，景氣溫和成長 ，通膨從高檔放緩 。若無重大地緣政治升
級事件，美元指數預期維持97–105區間震盪，並呈現「上有壓
力、下有支撐 」的盤整格局 。當地緣政治升溫時美元偏強 ，當
通膨回落與利率下降時美元偏弱。
資料來源： 中國信託商業銀行整理。2026/9

(1)匯市現況 : 美伊戰爭導致通膨疑慮再起，
美元走強
匯市現況: 2026年美元走勢主要受到美國經濟基本面、Fed貨幣
政策預期、川普政策以及地緣政治風險交互影響。
2026年初，川普重返貿易壁壘政策 、美國就業動能放緩且通膨
趨穩，市場普遍預期降息循環延續 ，使美元維持偏弱整理格局 ，
美元指數於1月27日一度跌至全年低點95.55。期間宣布華許出
任Fed主席的消息曾短暫帶動美元反彈 ，但整體仍未擺脫區間
震盪格局 。然而，2月28日伊朗事件爆發 ，3月9日西德州原油
一度接近每桶 120美元，能源價格上升帶動全球通膨預期升溫 ，
美國降息預期下降。進入3-4月，美國經濟數據進一步展現韌性，
製造業 、消費及投資表現改善 ，Fed季度預測亦同步上修 GDP

成長與通膨展望 。受此影響 ，利率期貨隱含年底前降息預期由
原先降息2碼逐步修正至不降息 ，推動美元於2Q26展開反彈。
同時 ，受油價飆升影響 ，9月Fed季度預測將今年 PCE上修至
3.7%，官員點陣圖亦上修利率路徑 ，市場開始討論通膨壓力可
能迫使Fed重新升息 。匯市焦點亦逐漸轉向新任聯準會主席華
許提出的新貨幣政策框架 。在Fed政策預期持續調整下 ，美元
指數於6月24日升至全年高點101.8，隨後進入高檔盤整格局。

重點提示
(2)政治議題：2026年共和黨失去絕對執政對
美元的影響(2/3)
數席次機率下行趨勢可見 ，當川普政策面無法政治上快速解決
問題並且影響民生時，取得參眾議院的機率加速下行。
期中選舉結果對美元影響 ：觀察1970年以來美國期中選舉結束
後一年美元之平均表現。
民主黨主導兩院 ：當民主黨期中選舉後在參眾議院均為多數 ，
美元指數平均下跌 -4.6%，6次有4次為下跌 ，且前一年美元均
下跌。1971、1987及2007年為下跌幅度最大的3年。
1971年尼克森(共和黨)總統宣布停止黃金兌換 ，10%進口附加
關稅，迫使盟友重啟貿易與貨幣談判(最終達成史密松寧協定)，
美元下跌-7.8%。

1987年雷根(共和黨)延續1985年廣場協議後美元貶值趨勢延續 。
雙赤字壓力 、通膨升溫 、利率上升以及外資拋售壓力導致發生
1987年10月19日黑色星期一股市崩盤 ，美元指數當年下跌 -
17.6%(1986年下跌-16.1%)。
2007年布希(共和黨)總統時期面臨雙赤字 、非美利差縮小 ，以
及次貸風暴風險升溫。美元下跌-8.3%(2006年下跌-8.2%)。

Thematic Report
重點提示
(2)政治議題：2026年共和黨失去絕對執政對
美元的影響(3/3)
共和黨、民主黨各主導一議院 ：當美國國會進入兩黨各主導一
議院時期，美元指數期中選舉過後一年平均上漲 3.6%，4次有3
次為上漲，且前一年4次美元均為上漲。美國期中選舉若造成國
會由不同政黨掌控 ，通常會限制總統隨意推動大規模擴張性財
政政策與赤字支出 。這類財政節制有助於降低通膨失控風險 ，
進而支撐美元走強。4次國會分裂時段其中較大美元升值趨勢為
1983年雷根(共和黨)時期。 1980年以來聯準會主席保羅伏克爾
採取強力升息手段打擊通膨 ，美國利率保持在相對高檔一段時
間且通膨已逐步受到控制 (從1980年15%降至4%以下)。美元

1983年升值14.9%(1982年升值12.1%)。1983年後，美國經濟
順利擺脫先前的嚴重衰退 ，展開強勁增長 ，市場對美國資產信
心大增，形成所謂強勢美元格局 。美元指數在80年代高點轉折
時期美國實質利率(基準利率-消費者物價指數)已從高檔下降。
小結：2026年期中選舉結果可能形成國會分裂 ，國會分裂長期
有助抑制赤字與通膨 ，因此長期對美元有利 ，但短期可能因預
算協商及債務上限僵局增加政治風險 ，對美元造成波動。2027
年將再度面臨美國債務上限 ，以及美伊地緣政治 、貿易戰的變
數均將牽動實質利率的變化 ，因此接下來將進一步分析選後影
響美元的關鍵因子。
✓
兩黨陷入僵局反而
對美元有利
✓
實質利率下降多半
隨美元趨勢走弱

Thematic Report
重點提示
(3)期中選後風險因子：財政、貿易戰、美伊戰
爭(1/3)
期中選舉過後影響美元的關鍵因子聚焦 財政面 (政府關門事件)、
貿易戰(法院裁定關稅無效後宣布以其他方式課徵關稅)、地緣政
治面(伊朗戰爭觸發油價上揚)。
財政面：8月國會通過持續撥款決議至 12月11日，代表政府關
門的風險將落在11月3日後，但新國會於2027年1月3日任職，
因此很可能選後再延後撥款決議 。除此之外 ，根據兩黨政策中
心預測，2027年9月後可能再度面臨美國債務上限議題 ，屆時
面臨2028年預算審核，參眾議院的分治恐延長達成共識的時間
以及提升美國主權債信評或展望調降風險 。從全國納稅人聯盟

(NTU)情境分析預測，美國聯邦政府收支很可能在2027年2月至
7月觸及債務上限。一旦債務達到法定限額，財政部長將宣布暫
停發行債務期 。在暫停發行債務期間內 ，財政部不得增加受法
定限額約束的債務 ，但到期債務仍可展期 。計算美國財政部透
過動用收入 、現有現金儲備和採取非常規措施 ，真正無法履行
聯邦義務約在2028年2月。然而債務上限和債務違約日(X-Date)
何時達到存在很大的不確定性 。受債務上限約束的債務將於
2027年3月接近法定上限 500億美元以內，之後隨著4月稅收的
到來而暫時下降 ，但若政府財政赤字增加超過原先預測 ，X-
Date將會提前到來。
